אֲבָל הַחִיצוֹנִית עַצְמָהּ לֹא נִתְקַּן עֲדַיִן בְּהַיִּחוּד כְּלָל וְהָיָה תָּלוּי וְעוֹמֵד עַד מַתַּן תּוֹרָה 

**but the external aspect itself had not yet been rectified in that *yichud* at all, and it remained suspended** in the sense that it was maintained in that state of separation between Yaakov and Israel, and was not fully consummated **until the Torah was given**  on Mount Sinai.**[^70]**

אִלּוּ שֶׁזָּכִינוּ לְהַלּוּחוֹת הָרִאשׁוֹנוֹת כִּי אָז הָיָה עָתִיד שֶׁיִּתְבַּטֵּל כֹּל הַזֻּהֲמָא כֻּלָּהּ 

**Had we been worthy of the first Tablets** which Moshe broke when some of the Israelites with the mixed multitude worshiped the golden calf and Moshe smashed the Tablets, **then the situation that will prevail in the future, when all** spiritual **contamination will be nullified,** would have become reality then,

וְהָיָה הַגִּלּוּי דְּהַיִּחוּד לְמַעְלָה וּלְמַטָּה שֶׁהוּא ה' אֶחָד וּשְׁמוֹ אֶחָד כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּהְיֶה לֶעָתִיד. 

**and the *yichud* would have been revealed above and below – that “God is One and His Name is One,” as it will be in the future.** 

ע' זוה"ק תְּרוּמָה קס"א ב' וְשָׁם קל"ד, א: 

**See *Zohar*** *II, **Terumah*** **161b; ibid. 134a.**

וְזֶהוּ הָעִנְיָן מָה שֶׁאַחַר שֶׁנִּקְרָא שְׁמוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל מֵתָה עָלָיו רָחֵל מִשּׁוּם שֶׁהִיא הָיְתָה מֶרְכָּבָה רַק לְהַתַּתָּאָה לְבַד וְכַנַ"ל 

**This is the insight as to why, after he was given the name Israel, Rachel passed away – because she was the *mercava* only to the lower level,**

וַהֲרֵי לֹא הָיָה נִתַּקֵּן עֲדַיִן הַחִיצוֹנִיּוֹת בְּיִחוּד הַתַּתָּאָה בְּעִלָּאָה 

**and the external aspect had not yet been rectified by means of the *yichud* of the lower level with the higher,** 

וְלָכֵן אַחַר שֶׁנִּתְעַלָּה יַעֲקֹב אָבִינוּ לְמַדְרֵגַת יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהוּא בְּחִי' הַגַּדְלוּת דז"א וְהוּא בְּאוֹר הַיִּחוּד 

**and therefore, after our Patriarch Yaakov ascended to the level of Israel, which is the aspect of *gadlut* of *z”a*** the full maturity of *ze’ir anpin*, when *ze’ir anpin* is fully endowed with *mochin* (*chochmah*, *binah* and *da’at*), **which is the light** **of that *yichud.***

לֹא הָיָה אֶפְשָׁר לְרָחֵל לְקַבֵּל אֶת הָאֹור הַזֶּה וּלְהִתְיַחֵד עִם יַעֲקֹב אָבִינוּ ע"ה עוֹד 

**But it was no longer possible for Rachel to receive this light and unite with our Patriarch Yaakov**

מִשּׁוּם שֶׁגּוּפָהּ שֶׁהוּא הַחִיצוֹנִית דְּאוֹר נִשְׁמָתָהּ לֹא הָיָה מְתוּקָן עֲדַיִן 

**since her body, which was the external aspect of the light of her soul, was not yet rectified.**

אֶלָּא רַק לִבְחִי' הַקְּטַנּוֹת וְלִהְיוֹת מֶרְכָּבָה לְהַתַּתָּאָה לְבַד בְּלִי יִחוּד בְּהַעִלָאָה 

**Instead, she was** still **at the level of *katnut*** spiritual immaturity, **and thus a *mercava* only for the lower level, without a *yichud* with the higher level.**

כִּי זֶהוּ הָעִנְיָן מָה שֶׁנִּתְחַלְּקוּ לד' נָשִׁים מִשּׁוּם שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה נִתְקַן הַחִיצוֹנִית לְמַטָּה בעוה"ז 

**This is the concept of them being divided into four wives – because the external aspect had not been rectified below in this world**

לִהְיוֹת מֶרְכָּבָה לְאוֹר הַיִּחוּד דְּהָעִלָּאָה בְּתַתָּאָה וּלְכָךְ נִתְחַלְּקוּ לד' גּוּפוֹת וְכַנַ"ל 

**so that it could serve as a *mercava* for the light of the *yichud* of higher and lower, and therefore they were divided into four bodies, as explained above.**

וְלָכֵן רָחֵל אֲשֶׁר הָיְתָה מֶרְכָּבָה לְמַטָּה לְהַתַּתָּאָה לְבַד וּבִלְתִּי הַגִּלּוּי דְּאוֹר הַיִּחוּד שֶׁהוּא בְּחִי' הַקַּטַנּוּת 

**Accordingly, since Rachel was the *mercava* below for the lower level only, and she was lacking the light of that *yichud* – which is the level of *katnut* –**


לֹא הָיָה אֶפְשָׁר לָהּ לְקַבֵּל אֶת הָאוֹר דְּשֵׁם יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהוּא בְּחִי' הַגַּדְלוּת 

**it was not possible for her to receive the light of the name Israel, which is the aspect of *gadlut*.**

לְכָךְ מֵאַחֲר שֶׁזָּכָה יַעֲקֹב אָבִינוּ ע"ה לְשֵׁם יִשְׂרָאֵל מִיתַת רָחֵל 

**Thus, after our Patriarch Yaakov became worthy of the name Israel, Rachel passed away,**

כִּי לֹא הָיָה אֶפְשָׁר לְגוּפָהּ שֶׁהוּא הַחִיצוֹנִית שֶׁבָּהּ לְהִתְיַחֵד עִמּוֹ עוֹד מִשּׁוּם שֶׁהַחִיצוֹנִית לֹא נִתַּקֵּן עֲדַיִן. 

**for it was impossible for her body – her external aspect – to unite with him again, since the external aspect had not yet been rectified.**

וְזֶהוּ הָעִנְיָן מָה שֶׁהָיָה כֹּל תְּשׁוּקַת יַעֲקֹב אָבִינוּ ע"ה רַק עַל רָחֵל 

**This is the insight as to why it was that all of Yaakov’s yearning was for Rachel exclusively.** 

מִשּׁוּם שֶׁבָּהּ תָּלוּי כָּל עִקָּר הַתִּקּוּן לְיַחֲדָהּ עִמּוֹ וּלְקָשְׁרָהּ לְמַעְלָה כִּי הוּא הָיָה מֶרְכָּבָה לז"א 

**Because the primary *tikun* – to unify her with him, and attach her above since he was the *mercava* for *ze’ir anpin* – depended on her.**

וְיִחוּד שְׁנֵיהֶם הוּא תִּקּוּן זו"ן מִשּׁוּם שֶׁהִיא הָיְתָה רַק בְּסוֹד הַקְּטַנּוֹת לְבַד וכמ"ש וְשֵׁם הַקְּטַנָּה רָחֵל

**The unification of the two of them** Yaakov and Rachel **would have been the *tikun* of *ze’ir anpin* and *nukvah*** (*malchut*), **because she was still only at the mystical level of *katnut*, as the verse** (Genesis 29:16) **states, “and the name of the smaller one** *haketana* i.e. the younger sister **was Rachel.”**

לָכֵן הָיָה רוֹצֶה לְתַקְּנָהּ תָּמִיד לְהַעֲלוֹתָהּ בְּסוֹד הַגַּדְלוּת וּכְמוֹ שֶׁזָּכָה הוּא לְשֵׁם יִשְׂרָאֵל 

**This is why he always longed to rectify her – to elevate her to the mystical secret of *gadlut* – just as he had been worthy of when he was given the name Israel.**

אַךְ מִשּׁוּם שֶׁמְּצִיאַת כָּל הָעוֹלָם כֻּלּוֹ שֶׁהוּא מִבְּחִי' הַנּוּק' הָאַחֲרוֹנָה דְּהַמַּלְכוּת הַכְּלָלִי אֲשֶׁר רָחֵל הָיָה מֶרְכָּבָה אֵלֶיּהָ

**But because the existence of the entire world was from the aspect of the final female element of macro-*malchut*, for which Rachel was the *mercava,***

לֹא הָיָה מְתוּקָן עֲדַיִן כְּלָל לָבוֹא לְמַדְרֵגַת הַיִּחוּד וּלְהָאִיר בָּאוֹר הַגַּדְלוּת 

**she was not rectified yet at all to be able to approach the level of *yichud* and shine with the light of *gadlut.***

כִּי הֲרֵי מִשּׁוּם זֶה נִתְחַלֵּק גִּלּוּיֵי דְּאוֹרוֹת הַנּוּקְבָּא לְמַטָּה בַּד' גּוּפוֹת כָּנִזְ' 

**This is why the revelations of the lights of *nukvah*** the female element **below was divided into four entities, as mentioned.**

וְהוּא מִשּׁוּם שֶׁהַחִיצוֹנִית דְּהָעוֹלָם הָיָה עֲדַיִן פָּגוּם מְאֹד ע"י קִלְקוּל כָּל הַדּוֹרוֹת כּוּלָּם וְכַנַ"ל 

**This is because the external aspect of the world was still deeply tainted by the destructive activities of all the** preceding **generations, as mentioned above.**

לָכֵן לֹא הָיָה אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁיְתּוּקָן רָחֵל גּוּפָהּ לָבוֹא לְמַדְרֵגַת הַיִּחוּד וְהַגַּדְלוּת 

**And therefore it was impossible for Rachel herself to be rectified and achieve the level of *yichud* and *gadlut.***

אֶלָּא אַדְּרַבָּה הָיָה שֶׁכַּאֲשֶׁר זָכָה יַעֲקֹב אָבִינוּ ע"ה לְשֵׁם יִשְׂרָאֵל נִסְתַּלְּקָה רָחֵל וּמֵיתָה:

**To the contrary, when Yaakov became worthy of the name Israel,** the spiritual aspect of **Rachel was retracted, and she passed away.**

אָמְנָם עכ"ז הִנֵּה נִתְקַיֵּם בְּיַעֲקֹב אָבִינוּ ע"ה לֹא יִגְרַע מִצַּדִּיק עֵינָיו (אִיּוֹב ל"ו) וְשֹׁרֶשׁ צַדִּיקִים יִתֵּן (מִשְּׁלֵּי י"ב) 

**However, even given all of the above, with our Patriarch Yaakov the verses (Job 36:7)** **“He will not remove His gaze from the righteous,” and (Proverbs 12:12) “The roots of the righteous will bear fruit” are fulfilled**

וְהוּא בַּמָּה שֶׁיָּלְדָה לוֹ אֶת יוֹסֵף וּבִנְיָמִין וְהֵם הָיוּ מִבְּחִי' יְסוֹד וַעֲטָרָה שֶׁבָּהּ וְכַנַ"ל אוֹת ז' 

**– in the sense that she** Rachel **bore him Yosef and Binyamin, who were from the aspect of *yesod* and its *atarah*** crown or corona, **as explained above subsection (g).**

וַהֲרֵי הֵם מֵהַקַּו הָאֶמְצָעִי שֶׁבָּהּ הָעוֹלֶה לְמַעְלָה לְמַעְלָה 

**They** Yosef and Binyamin **were from the middle column** of the *sefirot* **that ascends to the very heights** to *keter,***[^71]** 

וְהָיָה לִבּוֹ בָּטוּחַ בָּזֶה שֶׁהַגַּם שֶׁלֹּא זָכָה לְתַקֵּן אוֹתָהּ תֶּכֶף עכ"ז הֲרֵי נִשְׁאַר גִּזְעָהּ לְנֶצַּח עִמּוֹ וּתְתּוּקַּן לְבַסּוֹף עַל יָדוֹ עכ"פ 

**and his heart was sure that even though he had not merited to rectify her** Rachel **immediately, nevertheless, her ‘trunk’** so to speak, i.e. that which grows out of the roots, her progeny, **remained with him eternally, and would be rectified by him in the end.**

וַהֲרֵי יַעֲשֶׂה כֹּל תִּקּוּן הָעוֹלָם כּוּלּוֹ וְכֹל תִּקְוַת חֶמְדַּת יִשְׂרָאֵל רַק עַל יָדוֹ 

**Thus the entire *tikun* of the world would be accomplished, and the hope and yearning of the Jewish People would come about only through him,** 

וּכְמוֹ שֶׁאָמְרוּ רז"ל בָּבָא בָּתְרָא קכ"ג ע"ב רָאָה יַעֲקֹב אָבִינוּ שֶׁאֵין זַרְעוֹ שֶׁל עֵשָׂו נִמְסָר אֶלָּא בְּיַד זַרְעוֹ שֶׁל יוֹסֵף 

**as our Sages state in** Tractate ***Bava Batra*** **123b: ‘Yaakov foresaw that the progeny of Esau would be given over to the progeny of Yosef.**

וְכֵן הוּא בִּבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה פ' ע"ג סִימָן ז' ובכ"מ מָסֹרֶת הִיא שֶׁאֵין עֵשָׂו נוֹפֵל אֶלָּא בְּיַד בָּנֶיהָ שֶׁל רָחֵל 

**There is a similar statement in *Bereishit Rabba* 73:7, and in a number of other places: It is a tradition handed down that Esau will fall only into the hands of the children of Rachel.**

וְלָכֵן אָהַב אוֹתָם מִכֹּל בָּנָיו כִּי רַק בָּהֶם תָּלוּי כֹּל בִּטּוּל הָרַע כּוּלּוֹ 

**And this is why he** Yaakov **loved them more than all his other sons, for the termination of all evil depends only on them.**

וְהוּא מִשּׁוּם כִּי רָחֵל הִיא עִקָּרָהּ שֶׁל בַּיִת שֶׁשֹּׁרְשָׁה הוּא הַשְּׁכִינְתָּא תַּתָּאָה 

**And this is because Rachel, the primary wife of his household, was rooted in the lower level of the *Shechinah –*** 

שֶׁהִיא רָחֵל דְּאֲצִּילוּת הַמַּנְהֶגֶת אֶת כָּל הָעוֹלָמוֹת הַתַּחְתּוֹנִים 

**Rachel of Atzilut who directs** and guides **all of the lower worlds.**

וְכַאֲשֶׁר תְּתֻקַּן רָחֵל עִלָּאָה בְּיִחוּדָהּ לְמַעְלָה אָז יְתֻקַּן כֹּל הַחִיצוֹנִית דְּהָעוֹלָם ג"כ 

**Thus when the supernal Rachel will be rectified in her *yichud* up above, then the entire external aspect of the world will also be rectified**

בְּבִטּוּל כֹּל הָרַע ע"י בָּנֶיהָ שֶׁל רָחֵל לְמַטָּה וְיִהְיֶה כֹּל הַמְּצִיאוּת כּוּלּוֹ קוֹדֶשׁ לָה':

**with the termination of all evil through the agency of Rachel’s descendants below, and all of existence will be sanctified to God.**

וְהֵנָּה לֹא זָכְתָה רָחֵל לְהִבָּנוֹת מִיַּעֲקֹב אָבִינוּ ע"ה אֶלָּא רַק בְּיוֹסֵף וּבִנְיָמִין לְבַד 

**Now Rachel did not merit to be built up by Yaakov other than through Yosef and Binyamin,**

שֶׁהֵם מֵהַיְּסוֹד וְהָעֲטָרָה דְּרָחֵל דִּלְעֵלָּא שֶׁהוּא הַנְּקוּדָּה הַפְּנִימִית שֶׁבָּהּ הָעוֹלֶה לְמַעְלָה לְמַעְלָה 

**who were the *yesod* and the *atara* of the supernal Rachel – the innermost point within her that ascends to the loftiest heights –**

וְהוּא הַמַּלְכוּת שֶׁבָּהּ אֲשֶׁר בְּסוֹף הַקַּו הָאֶמְצָעִי 

**and this is the aspect of *malchut* within her, located at the end of the middle column,** 

הָעוֹלֶה עַד הַכֶּתֶר וְעַד הַמַּלְכוּת דא"ס וְכַנַ"ל אוֹת א' וְשָׁם הוּא כֹּל הַיִּחוּד דְּעִלָּא וּתְתָא

**that ascends to *keter*, and even to *malchut* of *Ein Sof*, as explained previously in subsection (a), and that is where the complete *yichud* of higher and lower takes place.**

וע"י שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה אָז הָעוֹלָם עֲדַיִן מְתֻקָּן כְּלָל לָכֵן לֹא זָכְתָה לְהִבָּנוֹת אֶלָּא רַק בְּהַנְּקֻדָּה הַפְּנִימִית שֶׁבָּהּ לְבַד מִשּׁוּם כִּי שָׁם הוּא הַיִּחוּד לְעוֹלָם וְהוּא קוֹצוֹ שֶׁל ד' דְּאַחַד.

**But since the world was not as yet rectified at all, she did not merit to be built up other than in her innermost point exclusively, because there the *yichud* is eternal and is** alluded to by **the ‘thorn’ atop the** letter ***dalet*** **of *echad*.**

אֲבָל עכ"פ הוּא כִּי עי"ז הִנֵּה יָרַד וְנִתְיַסֵּד בְּהָעוֹלָם כֹּל כֹּחַ הַתִּקּוּן כּוּלּוֹ 

**In any event, this was the way in which the entire potential for *tikun* descended into this world and became established.**

וְלָכֵן שָׁמַר אוֹתָם יַעֲקֹב אָבִינוּ ע"ה בְּיוֹתֵר מִכּוּלָּם וּכְמוֹ שֶׁרָאִינוּ בְּמָה שֶׁשָּׁם אֶת רָחֵל וְאֶת יוֹסֵף אַחֲרֹנִים 

**It was for this reason that our Patriarch Yaakov guarded them more than all the other** sons, **as we saw when he placed Rachel and Yosef behind everyone else** (see Genesis 33:2) when he saw Esau approaching in a menacing manner.

וְכֵן אָהַב אֶת יוֹסֵף וּבִנְיָמִין מִכֹּל בָּנָיו כִּי רְאֵה וְיֵדַע שֶׁהֵם יְסוֹד כֹּל הַתִּקּוּן דִּשְׁכִינְתָּא תַּתָּאָה בְּיִחוּדָהּ לְמַעְלָה 

**And he also loved Yosef and Binyamin more than his other sons because he saw and knew that they were the foundation of the entire rectification of the lower *Shechinah* in her *yichud* above,**

וּבָהּ תָּלוּי תִּקּוּן כֹּל הַמְּצִיאוֹת כּוּלָּהּ וְהוּא ע"י בָּנֶיהָ שֶׁל רָחֵל שֶׁהֵם מֵהַנְּקוּדָּה פְּנִימִית דְּהַשְּׁכִינְתָּא תַּתָּאָה 

**and it is upon this that the *tikun* of all of existence depends – by means of the sons of Rachel who are the innermost point of the lower level of *Shechinah,***

אֲשֶׁר תָּאִיר וְתַזְרִיחַ אוֹרָה לְמַעְלָה לְמַעְלָה לְיַחֵד הַכֹּל וּלְגָלָה כְּבוֹד מַלְכוּתוֹ יִתְבָּרֵךְ מִלְּעִילָא לְתַתָּא 

**whose light will illuminate and shine forth at the highest levels above, to unify everything and to reveal the glory of His blessed sovereignty from above to below.**

אֲשֶׁר זֶה הָיָה כַּוָּנַת הַמַּאֲצִיל ית"ש בְּכֹל בְּרִיאַת עוֹלָמוֹ 

**And this was the intention of the blessed Emanator in creating His entire universe.**

לָכֵן נָתַן יַעֲקֹב אָבִינוּ ע"ה כֹּל נַפְשׁוֹ רַק עַל רָחֵל לְתַקֵּן אוֹתָהּ 

**This was why Yaakov gave over his entire soul to Rachel exclusively, in order to rectify her.**

וְכֵן זָכָה לְבַסּוֹף לְהוֹלִיד מִמֶּנָּה אֶת יוֹסֵף וּבִנְיָמִין שֶׁהֵם יְסוֹד כֹּל הַתִּקּוּן הֶעָתִיד כָּנִזְ' 

**And in the end he did merit to bear with her Yosef and Binyamin, who are the foundation of the entire future *tikun,* as mentioned.**

וְזֶהוּ ג"כ מָה שֶׁאַחַר מִיתַת רָחֵל הֵסֵּיב מִטָּתוֹ בְּאֹהֶל בִּלְהָה 

**And this is also why, after Rachel passed away, Yaakov moved his bed into Bilhah’s tent**, and not into Leah’s tent,

מִשּׁוּם שֶׁהִיא הָיְתָה מֵהָאֲחוֹרַיִם דְּשֹׁרֶשׁ אוֹר דְּרָחֵל כַּנַ"ל 

**since she** Bilhah **was the external aspect of the root of Rachel’s light, as explained above,**

וְהָיָה נִצְרַךְ הִיא לְתִקּוּן הַרְבֵּה שֶׁהֲרֵי גַּם הַשְּׁבָטִים אֲשֶׁר נוֹלְדוּ מִמֶּנָּה שֶׁהֵם דָּן וְנַפְתָּלִי 

**and she needed extensive *tikun*, since even the tribes who were born to her, Dan and Naftali,**

הִנֵּה הֵם בְּהַתְּרֵין פִּרְקִין תַתָּאִיּן דִּיַרְכָא שְׂמָאלָא כַּנַ"ל אוֹת ז' וְכֹל צַד הַשְּׂמֹאל צָרִיךְ תִּקּוּן מְאֹד

**were from the two lower sections of the left leg**, so to speak, **as explained above subsection (g), and all of the left side required extensive *tikun*.**

וְלָכֵן הָיָה רוֹצֶה לְתַקֵּן אוֹתָהּ וּלְיַחֲדָה בְּאוֹר הַפָּנִים לְכָלְלָהּ בְּאוֹר אַחַת עִם הַפָּנִים בְּכֹל הָאֶפְשָׁרִי 

**And therefore he wanted to rectify her and unify her with the light of the front,** face-to-face so to speak, rather than only externally, back-to-back, **and include her as one light from the front as far as possible,**

וּכְמוֹ שֶׁתִּהְיֶה לֶעָתִיד כַּאֲשֶׁר יְתוּקַּן הַחִיצוֹנִית ג"כ שֶׁאָז יִתְאַחֲדוּ כּוּלָּן לְאַחַת מַמָּשׁ.

**as will be in the future when the external will be rectified as well, for at that time, they will all unite as one, literally.**

וְכֵן כֹּל הי"ב שְׁבָטִים לְאֶחָד וְכַנַ"ל אוֹת ז' 

**And similarly, the 12 tribes will unite as one, as explained above subsection (g).**

וְיִהְיֶה הַתְחָלַת הַתִּקּוּן מִבָּנֶיהָ שֶׁל רָחֵל מִשּׁוּם שֶׁהֵם מֵהַנְּקוּדָּה פְּנִימִית שֶׁל הַסּוֹף דְּקַו הָאֶמְצָעִי הָעוֹלֶה לְמַעְלָה לְמַעְלָה 

**The *tikun* will begin from Rachel’s sons, since they are the innermost point of the end of the middle column that ascends to the very heights.**

וְהוּא סוֹד הַיִּחוּד י' דְּיַעֲקֹב י' דְּיִשְׂרָאֵל י' דְּשָׁדֵי י' דַּאֲדֹנָי י' דַּהֲוָיָה 

**This is the mystical secret of the *yichud* of the *yud* of** the name **Yaakov; the *yud* of** the name **Israel; the *yud* of** the Name *Sh-adai***; the *yud* of** the Name ***Ad-nai,*** **and the *yud* of** the Name **Havaye** – all of these contain the letter *yud.*

וְהוּא סוֹד יוֹסֵף הַצַּדִּיק בְּרִית מִילָה שֶׁהוּא לְעֵדוּת בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל וְהוּא בְּקוֹצוֹ שֶׁל ד' דְּאַחַד 

**This is also the mystical secret of Yosef haTsadik** corresponding to **the circumcision of the male organ, a testimony in Israel** of our faith in the covenant, **and this is** alluded to **by the ‘thorn’ atop of the *dalet* of *echad.***

וְיָאֵר[^72] וְיִתְגַּלֶּה הַיִּחוּד לְעֵלָּא וּתְתָא וְיִהְיֶה ה' אֶחָד וּשְׁמוֹ אֶחָד:

**May this *yichud* shine forth and become revealed above and below, and “God will be One and His Name One”** as the verse in Zechariah 14:9 states.

[^1]:  The word **מִתְנַהֲגִים** implies both terms – deployed *and* directed.

[^2]:  Although the word **אוֹר** is not present here, in several other places in this chapter and elsewhere the author makes clear that we are talking about the *Ohr Ein Sof.*

[^3]:  The Hebrew term *pashut* can also be translated as ‘simple’ or ‘uncompounded.’

[^4]:  The parentheses were added in the author’s own handwriting (Makover). 

[^5]:  The word **מִתְנַהֲגִים** implies both terms – deployed *and* directed.

[^6]:  As a reminder, the light of *malchut* of *Ein Sof* is referred to as ‘the undefinable Infinite Light,’ whereas the light of *malchut* of Atzilut is referred to as G-dly Light or Divine Light.  

[^7]:  Part of the Introduction to the *Tikunei Zohar.*

[^8]:  Makover 

[^9]:  I.e. from the *malchut* of those *sefirot* (Makover).

[^10]:  I.e. even though the activities of *malchut* are in BiY”A, her root is in Atzilut.

[^11]:  From the outset *malchut* comprised only a single point, and the other nine aspects of *malchut* were emanated from that point (Makover).

[^12]:  As *nukvah* of *ze’ir anpin.*

[^13]:  Makover 

[^14]:  Traditionally, the origin of a man’s sperm is ultimately in his brain.

[^15]:  Meaning ‘be silent’

[^16]:  Literally ‘the radiant lens’ – referring to the *sefirah* of *tiferet* which illuminates the entire *partzuf* of *ze’ir anpin*, corresponding to the sun, whereas *malchut* is called ‘the dark lens’ *aspaklaria sh’eina me’ira* and corresponds to the moon which has no light of its own.  

[^17]:  It was Rachel who gave Leah the secret signs that Yaakov had given her to ensure that the wily Lavan would not deceive him and given him someone other than Rachel as a wife. Rachel did this to avoid Leah’s shame and embarrassment. See Genesis 29:17 ff. 

[^18]:  Although the Ba’al HaLeshem mentions six tribes here, he only lists five *sefirot*, excluding *yesod.* *Meir HaLeshem* explains that Yehuda doesn’t really belong on the list since he corresponds to *malchut*, so in reality there are only five tribes listed, and Yehuda was mentioned in error. However, the Arizal in *Sefer HaLikutim* (*Vayetze* 29\) explains that **Levi** is not counted among the Tribes (since the camp of the Levites and their camp were separated from the other Tribes and their camp after the Golden Calf) and so Yehuda took the place of the original Levite camp to the East, corresponding to *tiferet*. 

[^19]:  As the Ba’al HaLeshem will explain in ch. 3 the *atara* is the lowest aspect of *malchut* of that *partzuf*.

[^20]:  This is the way the word appear in the text, in the masculine form. It would seem that it should take the feminine form **שֶׁהִיא** but neither Makover nor Meir HaLeshem make note of this.

[^21]:  The actual question takes some time to get to. It begins with the words “**וא"כ הֲרֵי אֵיךְ נַעֲשׂוּ** – This being so, how did they become…” 

[^22]:  *‘Tikun’* in this context can also be rendered as ‘garment’, as in the verse (Deut. 22:5) **לֹא יִהְיֶה כְלִי גֶבֶר עַל אִשָּׁה וְלֹא יִלְבַּשׁ גֶּבֶר שִׂמְלַת אִשָּׁה אִשָּׁה** (a man’s attire should not be worn by a woman, and a man may not don women’s apparel), which the Targum renders as**לָא יְהֵי תִּקּוּן זֵין דִּגְבַר עַל אִתָּא וְלָא יִתַּקַּן גְּבַר בְּתִקּוּנֵי אִתָּא**, using the word *tikun* for ‘wear’ and ‘apparel.’ Thus, in order to become revealed, the *Shechinah* must be ‘dressed’ in the appropriate garments.

[^23]:  Ramak and commentaries.

[^24]:  Divine Inspiration. *Matok MiDevash*.

[^25]:  Makover.

[^26]:  Here is the question that the author began right at the beginning of the chapter, starting with the words: “We need to understand…”

[^27]:  Liturgy, part of the *Aleinu* prayer.

[^28]:  The gist of his explanation is that Yaakov made offerings there to the Divine Presence (the *Shechinah*) in the manner that the *Shechinah* had become revealed to Yitzchak.

[^29]:  No specific reference given, but probably in the same section on *Pinchas*.

[^30]:  *Anshei Knesset HaGedolah,* who established the content and order of the prayers. 

[^31]:  *Shir HaShrim Rabba* 2:2 (on the verse in Song of Songs 2:9).

[^32]:  In *Patach Eliyahu* (Intro, *Tikunei Zohar*) p. 19a.

[^33]:  E.g. *Zohar I*, 154a-b; *Zohar II*, 29b.

[^34]:  E.g. *Zohar II*, 29b.

[^35]:  She gave birth to 6 of the 12, and Yaakov’s other three wives each gave birth to only two. 

[^36]:  This is the way Yaakov himself referred to her – as *haketana*, the little on, or the minor one – in his conversation with Laban (Genesis 29:18).

[^37]:  Regarding this level the verse (Proverbs 5:5) states, “Her feet descend into death” since the *kelipot* derive sustenance from her there. See *Zohar I,* 35b. (Makover)

[^38]:  *Tikunei Zohar* p. 29b.

[^39]:  *Meir haLeshem.*

[^40]:  In the sense of diminished, contracted, reduced.

[^41]:  See above end of subsection (a). This is explained in *Shaarei Orah Sha’ar* 1\.

[^42]:  This is the Kabbalistic reading of the verse. The regular translation: “Forever will \[Your\] kindness be built.”

[^43]:  The numerical value of *chesed* (‘kindness’) is also 72, and is thus associated with the Name of 72\. 

[^44]:  Talmud Shabbat 33b. See commentaries there.

[^45]:  One of the stanzas in the *Lecha Dodi* hymn.

[^46]:  Another translation could be “to rule over all of Creation.”

[^47]:  See GRA *Tikunei Zohar* 199a (*Meir HaLeshem*).

[^48]:  *Pele* alludes to *keter*, which is secluded and concealed (*Meir HaLeshem* in the name of the GRA).

[^49]:  *Mufla* and *mechusa* – the former refers to *Arich Anpin*, the latter to *Atik Yomin* (*Meir HaLeshem* in the name of the GRA).

[^50]:  See e.g. *Etz Chaim* 28:1; *Sha’ar Ma’amarei Rashbi, Kedoshim*; *Zohar Harakiah, Vayechi*, *Mishpatim*; 

[^51]:  See *Ohr Hatorah, Bereishit* p. 432a ff.

[^52]:  See *Tikunei Zohar* p. 142b; GRA to *Sifra d’Tzinuita* 13b (Meir HaLeshem).

[^53]:  As was the case prior to the sin of the Tree of Knowledge, as explained in the previous chapter, and as will come about in the future (Makover).

[^54]:  כִּי נִכְסֹף נִכְסַפְתָּה לְבֵית אָבִיךָ'**’**

[^55]:  This is also found in *Pesikta d’Rav Kahana* 20:1.

[^56]:  Since she derived from *binah* (Makover).

[^57]:  ‘Necessary’ in the sense that once He decided that this is what He wanted, there is nothing that can stand in the way of the Divine Will.

[^58]:  See Rashi on the verse, from Midrash.

[^59]:  ‘Tikunim’ in this contest can be translated as ‘rectifications’ or as ‘garments,’ as mentioned above.

[^60]:  There seem to be some words missing here. Meir HaLeshem amends**כִּי** לִהְיוֹת מֶרְכָּבָה  to read **אָמְנָם** לִהְיוֹת מֶרְכָּבָה and we have translated it that way.

[^61]:  Ramak and commentaries.

[^62]:  *Yesod* should generally be translated as ‘foundation’ or basis. However, that would obscure the vital point here that *yesod* corresponds to the male and female organs, the organs of the lower *yichud* in particular. Accordingly we have left it as ‘*yesod’*.

[^63]:  *Yesod* should generally be translated as ‘foundation.’ However, that would obscure the vital point here that *yesod* corresponds to the male and female organs, the organs of the lower *yichud* in particular. Accordingly we have left it as ‘*yesod’*.

[^64]:  See previous footnote.

[^65]:  The implication seems to be that just as Rachel prevented the *yichud* as the ‘representative’ of the lower level of *Shechinah*, so too Leah prevented the *yichud* as the ‘representative’ of the higher level of *Shechinah*, and therefore she too should have passed on. (Makover)

[^66]:  See Ramak, *Matok MiDevash* and commentaries.

[^67]:  This is not actually a verse, but is mentioned as a statement in Tractate *Soferim* and in *Zohar I* 34a and is recited during the morning prayers. (*Meir haLeshem*).

[^68]:  This is a Talmudic expression indicating that something is far from practical and down-to-earth.

[^69]:  The abbreviation **סמ'** can be short for **סמאל** (Sama’el \- Satan) or for **סטרא דמסאבותא**, the side of impurity.

[^70]:  See Makover.

[^71]:  See *Zohar II* 175b.

[^72]:  Makover notes that this should probably be **וְיָאִיר**.
